```
1→import {
     2→  useEffect,
     3→  useRef,
     4→  useState,
     5→  useCallback
     6→} from 'react';
     7→import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
     8→import Joyride, { CallBackProps, STATUS, EVENTS, ACTIONS } from 'react-joyride';
     9→import { tourSteps } from './tourSteps';
    10→import {
    11→  getJoyrideAdvanceBehavior,
    12→  getSpotlightAdvanceBehavior,
    13→} from './stateMachine';
    14→import { useProductTour } from '@/shared/hooks/useProductTour';
    15→import { TOOL_ROUTES } from '@/shared/lib/toolRoutes';
    16→import { usePanes } from '@/shared/contexts/PanesContext';
    17→import { dispatchAppEvent } from '@/shared/lib/typedEvents';
    18→import { CustomTooltip } from './CustomTooltip';
    19→type ScheduleTimeout = (callback: () => void, delayMs: number) => ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;
    20→
    21→function useManagedTimeouts() {
    22→  const timeoutIdsRef = useRef<Set<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>>(new Set());
    23→
    24→  const scheduleTimeout = useCallback<ScheduleTimeout>((callback, delayMs) => {
    25→    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
    26→      timeoutIdsRef.current.delete(timeoutId);
    27→      callback();
    28→    }, delayMs);
    29→    timeoutIdsRef.current.add(timeoutId);
    30→    return timeoutId;
    31→  }, []);
    32→
    33→  const clearScheduledTimeouts = useCallback(() => {
    34→    for (const timeoutId of timeoutIdsRef.current) {
    35→      clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    36→    }
    37→    timeoutIdsRef.current.clear();
    38→  }, []);
    39→
    40→  useEffect(() => () => {
    41→    clearScheduledTimeouts();
    42→  }, [clearScheduledTimeouts]);
    43→
    44→  return { scheduleTimeout, clearScheduledTimeouts };
    45→}
    46→
    47→function pauseThenAdvance(
    48→  nextIndex: number,
    49→  setStepIndex: (value: number) => void,
    50→  setIsPaused: (value: boolean) => void,
    51→  delayMs: number,
    52→  scheduleTimeout: ScheduleTimeout,
    53→) {
    54→  setIsPaused(true);
    55→  scheduleTimeout(() => {
    56→    setStepIndex(nextIndex);
    57→    setIsPaused(false);
    58→  }, delayMs);
    59→}
    60→
    61→function dispatchTourEvent(name: 'openGenerationModal' | 'closeGenerationModal') {
    62→  dispatchAppEvent(name);
    63→}
    64→
    65→function useTourProgressState(isRunning: boolean, resetAllPaneLocks: () => void) {
    66→  const [stepIndex, setStepIndex] = useState(0);
    67→  const [isPaused, setIsPaused] = useState(false);
    68→  const hasInitializedRef = useRef(false);
    69→
    70→  useEffect(() => {
    71→    if (isRunning && !hasInitializedRef.current) {
    72→      hasInitializedRef.current = true;
    73→      setStepIndex(0);
    74→      setIsPaused(false);
    75→      resetAllPaneLocks();
    76→    } else if (!isRunning) {
    77→      hasInitializedRef.current = false;
    78→    }
    79→  }, [isRunning, resetAllPaneLocks]);
    80→
    81→  return { stepIndex, setStepIndex, isPaused, setIsPaused };
    82→}
    83→
    84→function useSpotlightClickAdvance(input: {
    85→  isRunning: boolean;
    86→  isPaused: boolean;
    87→  stepIndex: number;
    88→  setStepIndex: (value: number) => void;
    89→  setIsPaused: (value: boolean) => void;
    90→  setIsTasksPaneLocked: (locked: boolean) => void;
    91→  scheduleTimeout: ScheduleTimeout;
    92→}) {
    93→  const {
    94→    isRunning,
    95→    isPaused,
    96→    stepIndex,
    97→    setStepIndex,
    98→    setIsPaused,
    99→    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
   100→    scheduleTimeout,
   101→  } = input;
   102→
   103→  useEffect(() => {
   104→    if (!isRunning || isPaused) {
   105→      return;
   106→    }
   107→
   108→    const currentStep = tourSteps[stepIndex];
   109→    if (!currentStep?.spotlightClicks) {
   110→      return;
   111→    }
   112→
   113→    const target = document.querySelector(currentStep.target as string);
   114→    if (!target) {
   115→      return;
   116→    }
   117→
   118→    const handleClick = () => {
   119→      const nextIndex = stepIndex + 1;
   120→      const behavior = getSpotlightAdvanceBehavior(stepIndex);
   121→
   122→      if (behavior.lockTasksPane) {
   123→        setIsTasksPaneLocked(true);
   124→      }
   125→
   126→      if (behavior.delayMs) {
   127→        pauseThenAdvance(nextIndex, setStepIndex, setIsPaused, behavior.delayMs, scheduleTimeout);
   128→        return;
   129→      }
   130→
   131→      setStepIndex(nextIndex);
   132→    };
   133→
   134→    target.addEventListener('click', handleClick);
   135→    return () => target.removeEventListener('click', handleClick);
   136→  }, [isPaused, isRunning, scheduleTimeout, setIsPaused, setIsTasksPaneLocked, setStepIndex, stepIndex]);
   137→}
   138→
   139→function useJoyrideCallback(input: {
   140→  completeTour: () => void;
   141→  skipTour: () => void;
   142→  setIsGenerationsPaneLocked: (locked: boolean) => void;
   143→  setIsTasksPaneLocked: (locked: boolean) => void;
   144→  setStepIndex: (value: number) => void;
   145→  setIsPaused: (value: boolean) => void;
   146→  navigate: (path: string) => void;
   147→  scheduleTimeout: ScheduleTimeout;
   148→}) {
   149→  const {
   150→    completeTour,
   151→    skipTour,
   152→    setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
   153→    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
   154→    setStepIndex,
   155→    setIsPaused,
   156→    navigate,
   157→    scheduleTimeout,
   158→  } = input;
   159→
   160→  return useCallback((data: CallBackProps) => {
   161→    const { status, index, type, action } = data;
   162→
   163→    if (type === EVENTS.STEP_AFTER || type === EVENTS.TARGET_NOT_FOUND) {
   164→      const nextIndex = index + (action === ACTIONS.PREV ? -1 : 1);
   165→      const behavior = getJoyrideAdvanceBehavior(index, action);
   166→
   167→      if (behavior.type === 'advance') {
   168→        setStepIndex(nextIndex);
   169→      } else if (behavior.type === 'pause') {
   170→        if (behavior.dispatchEvent) {
   171→          dispatchTourEvent(behavior.dispatchEvent);
   172→        }
   173→        if (behavior.lockGenerationsPane) {
   174→          setIsGenerationsPaneLocked(true);
   175→        }
   176→        if (behavior.lockTasksPane) {
   177→          setIsTasksPaneLocked(true);
   178→        }
   179→        pauseThenAdvance(nextIndex, setStepIndex, setIsPaused, behavior.delayMs, scheduleTimeout);
   180→      } else if (behavior.type === 'waitForTarget') {
   181→        if (behavior.dispatchEvent) {
   182→          dispatchTourEvent(behavior.dispatchEvent);
   183→        }
   184→        if (behavior.releaseGenerationsPane) {
   185→          setIsGenerationsPaneLocked(false);
   186→        }
   187→        setIsPaused(true);
   188→        scheduleTimeout(() => {
   189→          const waitForTarget = () => {
   190→            const target = document.querySelector(behavior.selector);
   191→            if (target) {
   192→              setStepIndex(nextIndex);
   193→              scheduleTimeout(() => setIsPaused(false), behavior.resumeDelayMs);
   194→            } else {
   195→              scheduleTimeout(waitForTarget, behavior.resumeDelayMs);
   196→            }
   197→          };
   198→          waitForTarget();
   199→        }, behavior.delayMs);
   200→      } else {
   201→        const target = document.querySelector(behavior.selector) as HTMLElement | null;
   202→        target?.click();
   203→        pauseThenAdvance(nextIndex, setStepIndex, setIsPaused, behavior.delayMs, scheduleTimeout);
   204→      }
   205→    }
   206→
   207→    if (status === STATUS.FINISHED) {
   208→      completeTour();
   209→      navigate(TOOL_ROUTES.TRAVEL_BETWEEN_IMAGES);
   210→    } else if (status === STATUS.SKIPPED) {
   211→      skipTour();
   212→    }
   213→  }, [
   214→    completeTour,
   215→    navigate,
   216→    setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
   217→    setIsPaused,
   218→    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
   219→    setStepIndex,
   220→    scheduleTimeout,
   221→    skipTour,
   222→  ]);
   223→}
   224→
   225→export function ProductTour() {
   226→  const { isRunning, completeTour, skipTour } = useProductTour();
   227→  const { setIsGenerationsPaneLocked, setIsTasksPaneLocked, resetAllPaneLocks } = usePanes();
   228→  const navigate = useNavigate();
   229→  const { scheduleTimeout, clearScheduledTimeouts } = useManagedTimeouts();
   230→  const { stepIndex, setStepIndex, isPaused, setIsPaused } = useTourProgressState(
   231→    isRunning,
   232→    resetAllPaneLocks
   233→  );
   234→
   235→  useEffect(() => {
   236→    if (!isRunning) {
   237→      clearScheduledTimeouts();
   238→    }
   239→  }, [clearScheduledTimeouts, isRunning]);
   240→
   241→  useSpotlightClickAdvance({
   242→    isRunning,
   243→    isPaused,
   244→    stepIndex,
   245→    setStepIndex,
   246→    setIsPaused,
   247→    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
   248→    scheduleTimeout,
   249→  });
   250→
   251→  const handleCallback = useJoyrideCallback({
   252→    completeTour,
   253→    skipTour,
   254→    setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
   255→    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
   256→    setStepIndex,
   257→    setIsPaused,
   258→    navigate,
   259→    scheduleTimeout,
   260→  });
   261→
   262→  if (!isRunning) {
   263→    return null;
   264→  }
   265→
   266→  return (
   267→    <Joyride
   268→      steps={tourSteps}
   269→      run={isRunning && !isPaused}
   270→      stepIndex={stepIndex}
   271→      continuous
   272→      scrollToFirstStep
   273→      showSkipButton
   274→      showProgress
   275→      disableCloseOnEsc={false}
   276→      disableOverlayClose
   277→      callback={handleCallback}
   278→      tooltipComponent={CustomTooltip}
   279→      styles={{
   280→        options: {
   281→          zIndex: 100010,
   282→          arrowColor: 'hsl(var(--background))',
   283→        },
   284→        spotlight: {
   285→          borderRadius: 8,
   286→          transition: 'opacity 0.3s ease, transform 0.3s ease',
   287→        },
   288→        overlay: {
   289→          backgroundColor: 'hsl(0 0% 0% / 0.5)',
   290→          transition: 'opacity 0.3s ease',
   291→        },
   292→      }}
   293→      floaterProps={{
   294→        styles: {
   295→          floater: {
   296→            filter: 'drop-shadow(0 4px 12px hsl(0 0% 0% / 0.15))',
   297→            transition: 'opacity 0.3s ease, transform 0.3s ease',
   298→          },
   299→        },
   300→      }}
   301→    />
   302→  );
   303→}
   304→
```

```
    13→  if (error || !data) {
    14→    return null;
    15→  }
    16→
    17→  return data as GenerationRow;
    18→}
```

```
    10→interface LoraManagerProps extends LoraManagerOptions {
    11→  availableLoras: LoraModel[];
    12→  className?: string;
    13→  title?: string;
    14→  addButtonText?: string;
    15→  fullWidth?: boolean;
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * Realtime System Types
     3→ *
     4→ * Clean type definitions for the realtime system with clear contracts
     5→ * between layers (connection, processing, invalidation).
     6→ */
     7→
     8→// =============================================================================
     9→// Connection State Machine
    10→// =============================================================================
    11→
    12→export type ConnectionStatus =
    13→  | 'disconnected'
    14→  | 'connecting'
    15→  | 'connected'
    16→  | 'reconnecting'
    17→  | 'failed';
    18→
    19→export interface ConnectionState {
    20→  status: ConnectionStatus;
    21→  projectId: string | null;
    22→  error: string | null;
    23→  /** Timestamp when current status was entered */
    24→  statusChangedAt: number;
    25→  /** For reconnecting state: which attempt (1-based) */
    26→  reconnectAttempt: number;
    27→  /** For reconnecting state: when next retry will happen */
    28→  nextRetryAt: number | null;
    29→}
    30→
    31→export const INITIAL_CONNECTION_STATE: ConnectionState = {
    32→  status: 'disconnected',
    33→  projectId: null,
    34→  error: null,
    35→  statusChangedAt: Date.now(),
    36→  reconnectAttempt: 0,
    37→  nextRetryAt: null,
    38→};
    39→
    40→// =============================================================================
    41→// Raw Database Events (from Supabase)
    42→// =============================================================================
    43→
    44→export type DatabaseEventType = 'INSERT' | 'UPDATE' | 'DELETE';
    45→
    46→export type DatabaseTable =
    47→  | 'tasks'
    48→  | 'generations'
    49→  | 'shot_generations'
    50→  | 'generation_variants';
    51→
    52→/** Raw event from Supabase postgres_changes */
    53→interface RawDatabaseEventBase {
    54→  table: DatabaseTable;
    55→  eventType: DatabaseEventType;
    56→  /** Timestamp when event was received */
    57→  receivedAt: number;
    58→}
    59→
    60→interface RawInsertOrUpdateEvent<T = Record<string, unknown>> extends RawDatabaseEventBase {
    61→  eventType: 'INSERT' | 'UPDATE';
    62→  new: T;
    63→  old: Partial<T> | null;
    64→}
    65→
    66→interface RawDeleteEvent<T = Record<string, unknown>> extends RawDatabaseEventBase {
    67→  eventType: 'DELETE';
    68→  new: Partial<T> | null;
    69→  old: Partial<T> | null;
    70→}
    71→
    72→export type RawDatabaseEvent<T = Record<string, unknown>> =
    73→  | RawInsertOrUpdateEvent<T>
    74→  | RawDeleteEvent<T>;
    75→
    76→// =============================================================================
    77→// Typed Record Shapes (matching database schema)
    78→// =============================================================================
    79→
    80→export interface TaskRecord {
    81→  id: string;

    90→  created_at: string;
    91→  updated_at?: string;
    92→}

    96→  project_id: string;
    97→  type: string;

   100→  starred?: boolean;
```

```
1→/**
     2→ * RealtimeEventProcessor - Batches and normalizes raw database events
     3→ *
     4→ * Single responsibility: Take raw events, batch them within a time window,
     5→ * and emit processed events with normalized shapes.
     6→ *
     7→ * Does NOT make business decisions about what to invalidate - that's the
```

```
     4→  location: string;
     5→  thumbnail_url: string | null;
     6→  is_primary: boolean;
     7→}
     8→
     9→type GenerationRecord = Record<string, unknown> & {
    10→  id: string;
    11→  thumbnail_url?: string | null;
    12→};
    13→
    14→interface VariantGenerationLookupResult {
    15→  generation: GenerationRecord;
    16→  variantId: string;
    17→  variantIsPrimary: boolean;
    18→}
    19→
    20→interface SupabaseLike {
    21→  from: (table: string) => unknown;
    22→}
    23→
    24→export async function findGenerationByVariantLocation(
    25→  outputLocation: string,
    26→  supabaseClient: SupabaseLike,
    27→): Promise<VariantGenerationLookupResult | null> {
    28→  const { data: variantByLocation, error: variantError } = await (supabaseClient.from('generation_variants') as {
    29→    select: (columns: string) => {
    30→      eq: (column: string, value: string) => {
    31→        limit: (count: number) => Promise<{ data: VariantRow[] | null; error: unknown }>;
    32→      };
    33→    };
    34→  })
    35→    .select('id, generation_id, location, thumbnail_url, is_primary')
    36→    .eq('location', outputLocation)
    37→    .limit(1);
    38→
    39→  if (variantError || !variantByLocation || variantByLocation.length === 0) {
    40→    return null;
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  const variant = variantByLocation[0];
    44→  const { data: parentGen, error: parentError } = await (supabaseClient.from('generations') as {
    45→    select: (columns: string) => {
    46→      eq: (column: string, value: string) => {
    47→        single: () => Promise<{ data: GenerationRecord | null; error: unknown }>;
    48→      };
    49→    };
    50→  })
    51→    .select('*')
    52→    .eq('id', variant.generation_id)
    53→    .single();
    54→
    55→  if (parentError || !parentGen) {
    56→    return null;
    57→  }
    58→
    59→  const generation = parentGen;
    60→  return {
    61→    generation: {
    62→      ...generation,
    63→      location: variant.location,
    64→      thumbnail_url: variant.thumbnail_url || generation.thumbnail_url,
    65→    },
    66→    variantId: variant.id,
    67→    variantIsPrimary: variant.is_primary,
    68→  };
    69→}
```

## Summary

The file `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/config/testing/vitest.edge.config.ts` is **NOT orphaned**. It is actively referenced and used:

### 1. Package.json Script Reference
Line 28 in `package.json`:
```
"test:edge:unit": "vitest run --config config/testing/vitest.edge.config.ts",
```

### 2. What It Does
The file configures vitest for edge function unit tests. It:
- Defines patterns for edge function test files to include/exclude
- Sets up test environment, timeouts, and concurrency settings
- Uses shared helpers (`vitest.edge.shared.ts` and `vitest.edge.aliases.ts`) to reduce duplication
- Mocks external dependencies (Deno stdlib, Stripe, Supabase, Hugging Face, etc.)

### 3. No CI/CD or Workspace Config
- No `.github/workflows` files found in the repo
- No vitest workspace config exists
- The file stands alone as a dedicated edge test runner (separate from the main frontend tests in `vitest.config.ts`)

### 4. Related Files
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/config/testing/vitest.edge.smoke.config.ts` - separate smoke test config that also uses the shared helpers
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/config/testing/vitest.edge.shared.ts` - shared test config factory
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/config/testing/vitest.edge.aliases.ts` - shared module alias map (recently extracted to avoid duplication)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/supabase/functions/vitest.unit.config.ts` - this one IS orphaned and superseded
